- **mcpp**: Use for any hands-on work with the mcpp C++23 build tool - building, testing, gating, mcpp.toml configuration, workspaces, toolchains, cross-compilation, caching, build.mcpp/hooks, machine-readable output and CI wiring, or diagnosing a build that misbehaves. Also covers contributing to mcpp itself (issues, branch and commit conventions, self-hosted build, CI surface, release flow, upstream communication rules) and packaging a library into mcpp-index (descriptor .lua fields, features, GLOBAL+CN mirrors, lint rules, validate/publish CI, local verification, namespace conventions).
